Neon hot-swap: evict/lock are gated; see startup.log for [GOVERNOR]/[SWARM] lines.
Developer path (default): local offline operator loop — Reason+Act with REAL tool execution (read/write/execute); same doctrine as Neon Sovereign readme (agent product, not chat-only).
SDK path: SOVEREIGN_SDK_SINGLE_SHOT unset/0 => planReact (multi-step tools; incremental tasks may still force ReAct per autonomous_agent_impl.cpp).
SOVEREIGN_REACT_MAX_STEPS: unset/0/unlimited = no cap; else 4..50M bounds multi-step ReAct.
SOVEREIGN_REPO_ROOT=<dir> pins smoke-test under <dir>/smoke-test .
SOVEREIGN_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_SEC=<300-604800|0> idle stall budget (sec); default 3600; timer pauses while blackboard has active tasks. 0 or unlimited = wait until done.
SOVEREIGN_ARCHITECT_ASK_TIMEOUT_SEC=<120-7200> Logic-tier DAG wait per Architect task (default 1800).
SOVEREIGN_PROMPT_TIMEOUT_SEC=<30-7200> WinHTTP/Ollama wall clock per inference (default 1800; smoke integrity 1800 / fast 600 when SOVEREIGN_SMOKE_ARCHIVE is set unless this env overrides). Hung streams burn the full interval — see startup.log [GATEWAY] exceeded wall clock.
SOVEREIGN_SHADOW_LSP_HEADERS=0 skips LSP on include/*.hpp during smoke (bracket preflight only; default off in integrity smoke archives).
SOVEREIGN_CRUCIBLE_AUTO_REPAIR_ROUNDS=<1-32> inner repair attempts per submit_patch before swarm retry (default 8).
SOVEREIGN_CRUCIBLE_MAX_INNER_TOTAL=<1-512|0> per-task-id cumulative inner rounds across submit_patch calls (default 56 when SOVEREIGN_SMOKE_ARCHIVE is set; 0 = unlimited).
SOVEREIGN_CRUCIBLE_AUTHOR_REVIEW_PASSES=<0-8> author self-review passes before peer/Sentinel (integrity default 2).
SOVEREIGN_SMOKE_DEBUG_BLOB_MAX=<512-262144> caps smoke_debug.ndjson blob payloads (default 8192).
SOVEREIGN_SHADOW_CPP_TOOLCHAIN=auto|wsl|msvc — C++ shadow compile tries WSL g++ then MSVC vcvars cl on Windows.
SOVEREIGN_VCVARS_BAT=<path\\vcvars64.bat> overrides MSVC shadow detection (also probed at startup via vswhere).
SOVEREIGN_WSL_DISTRO=<name> pins the default WSL distro for agent tools (else Ubuntu, * default, first Linux).
SOVEREIGN_OLLAMA_NUM_PREDICT_LOGIC=<256-32768> caps Logic-tier generation tokens (default 8192; Architect DAG).
SOVEREIGN_PEER_GATE=0 disables Speed-tier peer review before Sentinel (integrity default: peer gate on).
SOVEREIGN_SMOKE_FAST=1 disables peer gate, integration link, and heavy shadow compile.
SOVEREIGN_SKIP_EXEC_MEETINGS=1 skips CEO/CPO/CTO inference hops (mandate_meetings.json not produced).
staff_mail.jsonl — NDJSON mirror of Staff Mail (CEO briefings + staff_mail_post tool); headless smoke exits before the UI event loop, so this file is the archive of operator-facing mail.
common_room.jsonl — NDJSON mirror of the shared software-house common room (SwarmChat + common_room_post + blackboard lifecycle); headless smoke archives team chat here.
agent_comms.jsonl — unified NDJSON log of common_room + DM traffic (also SOVEREIGN_AGENT_COMMS_LOG). SOVEREIGN_DM_MODERATION=1 holds new DMs pending until Architect dm_moderation_resolve.
Crucible peer/Sentinel/shadow cycle counters: [PIPELINE_METRICS] each poll; pipeline_telemetry.json overwritten each audit (partial) and again at terminal (final).
smoke_debug.ndjson — thread-safe NDJSON stream (Architect raw LLM + DAG node submits, blackboard, Crucible inner rounds / gates / commits). dag_malform.ndjson — Architect DAG parse/shape failures only.
SOVEREIGN_SMOKE_AUDIT_INTERVAL_SEC=<1-120> cadence for full task histogram + [PIPELINE_METRICS] block (default 5).
SOVEREIGN_SMOKE_TICK_LOG=1|true — append smoke_poll_tick each simulated second (very verbose).
SOVEREIGN_VRAM_SWAP_NEURAL_IDLE_SEC=<60-7200> max wait (sec) for neural drain before Logic unload at Sentinel barrier; headless smoke defaults 600 if unset (0 = wait forever, can wedge if Ollama hangs).
SOVEREIGN_SMOKE_SKIP_VRAM_BARRIER=1 — skip Logic/Speed VRAM evict+lock when both tiers fit in VRAM; unset/0 keeps the barrier for sequential residency on tight GPUs.
